## Decision: yes, do the integration now

Phase 3 is closed, and Phase 4's core is done: embeddings, calibration and the own-voice test all work. The remaining Phase 4 work (extra volunteers) doesn't affect the code. Doing a thin integration now exposes memory and latency problems early, and it lets us collect real score logs for the fusion network.

## Reading your own-voice test

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Enrolment | 27 windows from 9 clips |
| Your clean clips | mean similarity 0.80, min 0.74, **0% below threshold** |
| Your far-field clips | mean **0.58**, min **0.37**, 0% below threshold |
| Impostors (40 LibriSpeech speakers, 120 trials) | mean 0.09, max 0.25, 0% above the 0.298 threshold |
| EER | 0.00% |

The far-field drop (0.80 to 0.58, with the worst window only 0.07 above the threshold) is the one to watch: a genuine relative on a poor speakerphone line could be wrongly flagged as a stranger. And "0.00%" means no errors seen in 54 target and 120 impostor trials. Statistically that is still up to about 5% on the target side. The impostors are clean English readers, not relatives or voice clones, so this is a necessary test, not a sufficient one.

## What I built, and what I verified

| File | What | Verified how |
|---|---|---|
| `backend/main.py` (replace) | Runs spoof, speech-to-text plus intent, and (if a contact is chosen) voice, in parallel threads, and returns the unified message | Tested with stand-in models over a real WebSocket |
| `frontend/src/App.jsx` (replace) | Transcript, four signal chips, voice-match bar and contact dropdown, spoof gauge, latency line | Syntax-checked only (the page itself not run) |
| `voice/enroll_cli.py` (new) | Command-line enrolment with a consent prompt; stores only the embedding | Syntax-checked only |

**What the protocol tests confirmed:** `/health` and `/contacts` work; a window returns spoof and intent only; selecting an unknown contact gives an error; selecting an enrolled one adds the voice stream; pure silence returns `{"silent": true}` without calling any model; and when a window arrives while the previous one is still being scored, it is skipped and counted. **What I could not test:** the real models, the GPU, a microphone and the React page. Treat first run as a debugging session.

## Step 1: Replace `backend/main.py`

```python
import asyncio, json, time
import numpy as np
from fastapi import FastAPI, WebSocket
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from audio.spoof_stream import score_chunk
from nlp.asr import transcribe
from nlp.intent_stream import score_text
from voice.voice_stream import score_voice, list_enrolled
from backend.chunker import RingBuffer

SILENCE_RMS = 0.005                      # windows quieter than this are not sent to any model
NEUTRAL_INTENT = {"stream": "intent", "score": 0.0, "labels": {}, "evidence": []}

app = FastAPI()
app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=["*"], allow_methods=["*"], allow_headers=["*"])


@app.get("/health")
def health():
    return {"ok": True}


@app.get("/contacts")
def contacts():
    return {"contacts": list_enrolled()}


def _timed(fn, *args):
    t0 = time.time()
    out = fn(*args)
    return out, int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)


def _intent_pipeline(window):
    text, t_asr = _timed(transcribe, window)
    if not text.strip():
        return text, NEUTRAL_INTENT, t_asr, 0
    res, t_int = _timed(score_text, text)
    return text, res, t_asr, t_int


async def _process(ws, window, state):
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        if float(np.sqrt(np.mean(window ** 2))) < SILENCE_RMS:
            await ws.send_json({"t": time.time(), "silent": True})
            return
        contact = state["contact"]
        jobs = [loop.run_in_executor(None, _timed, score_chunk, window),
                loop.run_in_executor(None, _intent_pipeline, window)]
        if contact:
            jobs.append(loop.run_in_executor(None, _timed, score_voice, window, contact))
        res = await asyncio.gather(*jobs)
        (spoof, t_spoof), (text, intent, t_asr, t_int) = res[0], res[1]
        streams = {"spoof": {"stream": "spoof", "score": round(float(spoof), 4)}, "intent": intent}
        lat = {"spoof": t_spoof, "asr": t_asr, "intent": t_int}
        if contact:
            voice, t_voice = res[2]
            streams["voice"] = voice
            lat["voice"] = t_voice
        lat["total"] = int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)
        await ws.send_json({"t": time.time(), "window_s": 4, "transcript": text, "streams": streams,
                            "latency_ms": lat, "dropped_windows": state["dropped"]})
    except Exception as e:                                   # never freeze the UI on a model error
        await ws.send_json({"type": "error", "detail": f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"})
    finally:
        state["busy"] = False


@app.websocket("/ws")
async def ws_endpoint(ws: WebSocket):
    await ws.accept()
    buf = RingBuffer()
    state = {"busy": False, "contact": None, "dropped": 0}
    task = None
    try:
        while True:
            m = await ws.receive()
            if m["type"] == "websocket.disconnect":
                break
            if m.get("bytes") is not None:
                window = buf.push(np.frombuffer(m["bytes"], dtype=np.float32))
                if window is None:
                    continue
                if state["busy"]:
                    state["dropped"] += 1                    # previous window still being scored: skip this one
                    continue
                state["busy"] = True
                task = asyncio.create_task(_process(ws, window, state))
            elif m.get("text") is not None:
                try:
                    ctrl = json.loads(m["text"])
                except ValueError:
                    continue
                if ctrl.get("type") == "select_contact":
                    name = ctrl.get("name") or None
                    if name is None or name in list_enrolled():
                        state["contact"] = name
                        await ws.send_json({"type": "contact_set", "name": name})
                    else:
                        await ws.send_json({"type": "error", "detail": f"'{name}' is not enrolled"})
    finally:
        if task is not None and not task.done():
            task.cancel()
        buf.clear()
```
Keep `backend/chunker.py` as it is. No transcript or audio is logged: the transcript only travels to the page and is discarded.

## Step 2: Replace `frontend/src/App.jsx`

```jsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";

const API = "http://localhost:8000";
const WS = "ws://localhost:8000/ws";
const LABELS = ["urgency", "authority", "otp_pii", "payment"];
const NICE = { urgency: "Urgency", authority: "Authority claim", otp_pii: "OTP / PIN / card request", payment: "Payment request" };

function Gauge({ value, color }) {
  const pct = Math.round((value ?? 0) * 100);
  return (
    <div style={{ background: "#eee", height: 22, borderRadius: 11 }}>
      <div style={{ width: `${pct}%`, height: "100%", background: color, borderRadius: 11, transition: "width .4s" }} />
    </div>
  );
}

export default function App() {
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState(null);
  const [silent, setSilent] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState(null);
  const [contacts, setContacts] = useState([]);
  const [contact, setContact] = useState("");
  const ref = useRef({});
  const contactRef = useRef("");

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch(`${API}/contacts`).then((r) => r.json()).then((d) => setContacts(d.contacts || [])).catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  function sendContact(name) {
    const ws = ref.current.ws;
    if (ws && ws.readyState === 1) ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "select_contact", name: name || null }));
  }
  function onContactChange(e) {
    setContact(e.target.value);
    contactRef.current = e.target.value;
    sendContact(e.target.value);
  }

  async function start() {
    setError(null);
    const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
      audio: { echoCancellation: false, noiseSuppression: false, autoGainControl: false },
    });
    const ctx = new AudioContext({ sampleRate: 16000 });
    await ctx.audioWorklet.addModule("/processor.js");
    const src = ctx.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
    const node = new AudioWorkletNode(ctx, "pcm-processor");
    const ws = new WebSocket(WS);
    ws.binaryType = "arraybuffer";
    ws.onopen = () => sendContact(contactRef.current);
    ws.onmessage = (e) => {
      const m = JSON.parse(e.data);
      if (m.type === "error") { setError(m.detail); return; }
      if (m.type === "contact_set") return;
      if (m.silent) { setSilent(true); return; }
      setSilent(false); setError(null); setMsg(m);
    };
    node.port.onmessage = (e) => { if (ws.readyState === 1) ws.send(e.data); };
    src.connect(node);
    ref.current = { stream, ctx, ws };
    setRunning(true);
  }

  function stop() {
    const { stream, ctx, ws } = ref.current;
    stream?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
    ws?.close(); ctx?.close();
    setRunning(false);
  }

  const s = msg?.streams;
  const intent = s?.intent;
  const spoof = s?.spoof?.score ?? 0;
  const voice = s?.voice;
  const fired = new Set(intent?.evidence || []);

  return (
    <div style={{ maxWidth: 520, margin: "40px auto", fontFamily: "sans-serif", padding: "0 12px" }}>
      <h2>RakshaVoice</h2>

      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12, alignItems: "center", flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
        <button onClick={running ? stop : start}>{running ? "Stop" : "Start listening"}</button>
        <label>
          Caller claims to be:{" "}
          <select value={contact} onChange={onContactChange}>
            <option value="">nobody in particular</option>
            {contacts.map((c) => <option key={c} value={c}>{c}</option>)}
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>

      {error && <p style={{ color: "#b00" }}>Server: {error}</p>}
      {running && silent && <p style={{ color: "#777" }}>Listening… (silence)</p>}

      <h4 style={{ marginBottom: 6 }}>What the caller is saying</h4>
      <div style={{ minHeight: 44, padding: 8, background: "#f6f6f6", borderRadius: 6 }}>{msg?.transcript || "…"}</div>

      <h4 style={{ marginBottom: 6 }}>Scam signals</h4>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", flexWrap: "wrap", gap: 8 }}>
        {LABELS.map((l) => {
          const v = intent?.labels?.[l];
          const on = fired.has(l);
          return (
            <span key={l} style={{ padding: "6px 10px", borderRadius: 16, fontSize: 14,
                                   background: on ? "#d33" : "#e9e9e9", color: on ? "#fff" : "#555" }}>
              {NICE[l]}{v !== undefined ? ` ${Math.round(v * 100)}%` : ""}
            </span>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <h4 style={{ marginBottom: 6 }}>Voice check</h4>
      {voice ? (
        <div>
          <Gauge value={voice.score} color={voice.evidence?.length ? "#d33" : "#2e9e5b"} />
          <p style={{ margin: "6px 0" }}>
            {voice.evidence?.length
              ? `This voice does NOT match ${voice.enrolled} (similarity ${voice.similarity})`
              : `Matches ${voice.enrolled} (similarity ${voice.similarity})`}
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p style={{ color: "#777", margin: "6px 0" }}>Choose who the caller claims to be to compare voices.</p>
      )}

      <h4 style={{ marginBottom: 6 }}>Synthetic-voice score (not yet calibrated)</h4>
      <Gauge value={spoof} color={spoof < 0.4 ? "#2e9e5b" : spoof < 0.7 ? "#e0a100" : "#d33"} />

      {msg?.latency_ms && (
        <p style={{ color: "#777", fontSize: 13 }}>
          Total {msg.latency_ms.total} ms (spoof {msg.latency_ms.spoof}, speech-to-text {msg.latency_ms.asr}, intent {msg.latency_ms.intent}
          {msg.latency_ms.voice !== undefined ? `, voice ${msg.latency_ms.voice}` : ""}) · skipped windows: {msg.dropped_windows}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```
`public/processor.js` stays unchanged. The spoof gauge is labelled "not yet calibrated" on purpose: your Phase 1 findings showed live audio scores high, and calibrating it is a fusion and evaluation job.

## Step 3: Enrol yourself (consent built in)

Save as `voice/enroll_cli.py`, then run `python -m voice.enroll_cli` from `rakshavoice/`. It shows the consent statement, requires you to type `YES`, records three 8-second clips (kept in memory only), and saves just the averaged embedding. `--delete name` removes a person.

```python
import argparse
import sounddevice as sd
from voice.voice_stream import enroll, list_enrolled, delete_enrolled

SR, N_CLIPS, SEC = 16000, 3, 8
CONSENT = ("I agree that RakshaVoice may store a numerical voiceprint (not my audio) made from 3 short recordings of my voice, "
           "on this device only, to check whether a caller is me. I can ask for it to be deleted at any time.")

ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(); ap.add_argument("--delete"); args = ap.parse_args()
if args.delete:
    delete_enrolled(args.delete.strip().lower()); print("Deleted. Enrolled now:", list_enrolled()); raise SystemExit

name = input("Name for this contact (for example: mom): ").strip().lower()
print("\nConsent statement. Read it to the person being enrolled:\n\n  " + CONSENT + "\n")
if input("Does the person agree? Type YES to continue: ").strip() != "YES":
    raise SystemExit("Not enrolled.")
wavs = []
for i in range(N_CLIPS):
    input(f"Clip {i + 1}/{N_CLIPS}: press Enter, then speak naturally for {SEC} seconds ...")
    y = sd.rec(int(SEC * SR), samplerate=SR, channels=1, dtype="float32"); sd.wait()
    wavs.append(y[:, 0])                                   # audio stays in memory only; just the averaged embedding is saved
enroll(name, wavs, CONSENT)
print("Enrolled:", list_enrolled())
```

## Step 4: A memory escape hatch (two one-line edits)
Four models together measured about 3.4 GB before adding ECAPA, so the whole server may be tight. Make the device configurable so you can move a small model to the CPU without editing code. In `voice/voice_stream.py`, add `import os` at the top and change the `DEV` line to:
```python
DEV = os.environ.get("RV_VOICE_DEVICE") or ("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
```
In `nlp/intent_stream.py`, add `import os` and change the `DEV` line to:
```python
DEV = os.environ.get("RV_INTENT_DEVICE") or ("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
```
If the GPU runs out of memory, start the server in PowerShell with `$env:RV_VOICE_DEVICE="cpu"; uvicorn backend.main:app --port 8000` (the voice model is small on CPU).

## Step 5: Run it

1. Close Edge, Brave and WhatsApp. Start the server from `rakshavoice/`: `uvicorn backend.main:app --port 8000`. Startup will take a while (four models). Note how long, and watch `nvidia-smi` once it says "Application startup complete".
2. In `frontend/`: `npm run dev`, then open the page in Chrome.
3. Start listening and read an English scam line ("Please tell me the OTP you just received"). You should see the transcript and the `OTP / PIN / card request` chip turn red within a few seconds.
4. After enrolling yourself (name it "me"), reload the page, pick "me" in the dropdown, and speak: expect "Matches me". Then play a clip of someone else (a LibriSpeech file through your speakers) and expect "does NOT match".
5. Watch the "skipped windows" counter after 30 seconds of speech. If it climbs steadily, the pipeline is slower than the 2 s hop, and I'll help tune it.

## What this does not do yet
There is no combined risk score, no explanation sentence and no challenge question. Those are the fusion network (Phase 5) and the GenAI agents (Phase 6). For now the page just shows each stream's evidence side by side.

4. Whether the voice check says "Matches me" for you and "does NOT match" for another voice.

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import "./App.css";

const WS_URL = "ws://127.0.0.1:8000/ws";

export default function App() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("Disconnected");
  const [score, setScore] = useState(null);
  const [latency, setLatency] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [recording, setRecording] = useState(false);
  const [fileResult, setFileResult] = useState(null);
  const [fileLoading, setFileLoading] = useState(false);
  const [fileError, setFileError] = useState("");

  const wsRef = useRef(null);
  const audioContextRef = useRef(null);
  const sourceRef = useRef(null);
  const processorRef = useRef(null);
  const streamRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      stopAudio();
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  async function startAudio() {
    try {
      setError("");
      setScore(null);
      setLatency(null);
      setStatus("Requesting microphone…");

      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        audio: {
          channelCount: 1,
          echoCancellation: false,
          noiseSuppression: false,
          autoGainControl: false,
        },
      });
      streamRef.current = stream;

      const audioContext = new AudioContext({ sampleRate: 16000 });
      audioContextRef.current = audioContext;

      console.log("Browser audio sample rate:", audioContext.sampleRate);

      const ws = new WebSocket(WS_URL);
      ws.binaryType = "arraybuffer";
      wsRef.current = ws;

      ws.onopen = async () => {
        try {
          setStatus("Connected — listening");
          setRecording(true);

          const source = audioContext.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
          sourceRef.current = source;

          await audioContext.audioWorklet.addModule("/processor.js");

          const processor = new AudioWorkletNode(
            audioContext,
            "pcm-processor"
          );
          processorRef.current = processor;

          processor.port.onmessage = (event) => {
            if (ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;

            ws.send(event.data);
          };

          source.connect(processor);

          // Keep the audio graph active without playing microphone audio
          // through the speakers.
          const mute = audioContext.createGain();
          mute.gain.value = 0;
          processor.connect(mute);
          mute.connect(audioContext.destination);
        } catch (err) {
          setError(err.message || "Could not initialize audio processing.");
          setStatus("Unable to start");
          await stopAudio();
        }
      };

      ws.onerror = () => {
        setError("WebSocket connection failed. Check that FastAPI is running.");
        setStatus("Connection error");
      };

      ws.onclose = () => {
        setStatus("Disconnected");
        setRecording(false);
      };

      ws.onmessage = (event) => {
        try {
          const result = JSON.parse(event.data);
          if (result.stream === "spoof") {
            setScore(result.score);
            setLatency(result.latency_ms);
          }
        } catch {
          setError("Received an invalid response from the backend.");
        }
      };
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Could not start microphone.");
      setStatus("Unable to start");
      await stopAudio();
    }
  }

  
  async function stopAudio() {
    setRecording(false);

    if (processorRef.current) {
      processorRef.current.port.onmessage = null;
      processorRef.current.disconnect();
      processorRef.current = null;
    }

    if (sourceRef.current) {
      sourceRef.current.disconnect();
      sourceRef.current = null;
    }

    if (wsRef.current) {
      wsRef.current.onclose = null;

      if (
        wsRef.current.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN ||
        wsRef.current.readyState === WebSocket.CONNECTING
      ) {
        wsRef.current.close();
      }

      wsRef.current = null;
    }

    if (streamRef.current) {
      streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
      streamRef.current = null;
    }

    if (audioContextRef.current) {
      await audioContextRef.current.close().catch(() => {});
      audioContextRef.current = null;
    }

    setStatus("Disconnected");
  }

  
  async function analyzeFile(event) {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setFileResult(null);
    setFileError("");

    
    if (!/\.(wav|ogg|oga|opus)$/i.test(file.name)) {
      setFileError("Please select a WAV, OGG, OGA, or OPUS audio file.");
      event.target.value = "";
      return;
    }


    setFileLoading(true);

    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append("file", file);

      const response = await fetch(
        "http://127.0.0.1:8000/analyze-file",
        {
          method: "POST",
          body: formData,
        }
      );

      const data = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.detail || "Audio analysis failed.");
      }

      setFileResult(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setFileError(
        err.message || "Could not connect to the backend."
      );
    } finally {
      setFileLoading(false);
      event.target.value = "";
    }
  }

  const percent = score == null ? null : Math.round(score * 10000) / 100;

  return (
    <main className="app">
      <header className="header">
        <div className="brand">
          <div className="brand-icon">RV</div>
          <div>
            <h1>RakshaVoice</h1>
            <p>Real-time audio authenticity monitoring</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className={`status ${recording ? "active" : ""}`}>
          <span className="status-dot" />
          {status}
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className="hero">
        <p className="eyebrow">AUDIO SECURITY · LIVE ANALYSIS</p>
        <h2>Detect suspicious audio in real time.</h2>
        <p className="subtitle">
          Analyze microphone audio using your trained Wav2Vec2-based spoof
          classifier. Audio is streamed to your local backend for inference.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section className="dashboard">
        <article className="panel score-panel">
          <p className="panel-label">LATEST SPOOF SCORE</p>
          <div className="score">
            {percent == null ? "—" : `${percent.toFixed(2)}%`}
          </div>
          <p className="muted">
            {score == null
              ? "Start monitoring to receive a score"
              : score >= 0.5
                ? "Higher predicted spoof probability"
                : "Lower predicted spoof probability"}
          </p>
          <div className="meter">
            <div
              className="meter-fill"
              style={{ width: `${percent == null ? 0 : percent}%` }}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="meter-labels">
            <span>0% · Lower</span>
            <span>100% · Higher</span>
          </div>
        </article>

        <article className="panel">
          <p className="panel-label">INFERENCE LATENCY</p>
          <div className="metric">
            {latency == null ? "—" : `${latency.toFixed(1)} ms`}
          </div>
          <p className="muted">Time spent scoring the latest audio window</p>
        </article>

        <article className="panel">
          <p className="panel-label">CONNECTION</p>
          <div className="metric metric-small">
            {recording ? "Live" : "Idle"}
          </div>
          <p className="muted">Local FastAPI WebSocket</p>
          <p className="endpoint">{WS_URL}</p>
        </article>
      </section>

      <section className="panel controls">
        <div>
          <h3>Live microphone analysis</h3>
          <p className="muted">
            Allow microphone access when your browser asks. Keep speaking to
            receive successive audio-window scores.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="buttons">
          {!recording ? (
            <button className="primary" onClick={startAudio}>
              Start monitoring
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button className="danger" onClick={stopAudio}>
              Stop monitoring
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        {error && <p className="error">{error}</p>}
      </section>

      
      <section className="panel controls file-analysis">
        <div>
          <h3>Analyze an audio file</h3>
          <p className="muted">
            Upload a WAV recording to analyze it with the trained
            RakshaVoice spoof classifier.
          </p>
        </div>

        <input
          type="file"
          accept=".wav,.ogg,.oga,.opus,audio/*"
          onChange={analyzeFile}
          disabled={fileLoading}
        />

        {fileLoading && <p>Analyzing audio...</p>}

        {fileError && <p className="error">{fileError}</p>}

        {fileResult && (
          <div className="file-result">
            <h3>Analysis result</h3>
            <p>
              <strong>File:</strong> {fileResult.filename}
            </p>
            <p>
              <strong>Duration:</strong>{" "}
              {fileResult.duration_seconds} seconds
            </p>
            <p>
              <strong>Spoof probability:</strong>{" "}
              {fileResult.spoof_probability_percent}%
            </p>
            <p>
              <strong>Sample rate:</strong>{" "}
              {fileResult.sample_rate} Hz
            </p>
            <p className="muted">
              Higher scores indicate higher predicted spoof probability.
              This is a model prediction, not a definitive verdict.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>


      <footer>
        <p>
          Scores are model predictions, not proof that a voice is authentic or
          spoofed. Validate against known audio before relying on decisions.
        </p>
        <p>RakshaVoice · Local prototype</p>
      </footer>
    </main>
  );
}

I kept your design and built on it. The earlier App.jsx I gave you is discarded; use this one. It keeps all your existing classes, the microphone and file-upload code, the mute-gain trick and the cleanup logic exactly as they were. New panels reuse your `panel`, `panel-label`, `meter`, `metric` and `muted` classes, so they pick up your styling.

## Read this before changing the backend

Your page calls `http://127.0.0.1:8000/analyze-file` for file upload, but the backend I gave you last time doesn't have that endpoint. **If you replace `backend/main.py` with my version, the file-upload section will stop working.** I can't see your current `main.py`, so please paste it (or just the `/analyze-file` function and its imports) and I'll merge both into one file. Until then, keep your current backend.

The new page also works with your **current** backend: it still understands the old spoof-only message, so you can try the look right away. The new panels fill in once the merged backend is running.

## What changed in the page

| Area | Change |
|---|---|
| Header, hero, three top panels | Same layout. The latency panel now also shows the per-stage breakdown, and the connection panel shows skipped windows |
| New panel: **What the caller is saying** | Live transcript, plus the four **scam signal chips** (they turn red when a signal fires) |
| New panel: **Voice check** | Dropdown "The caller says they are…" with enrolled contacts, a match / no-match status and bar |
| Messages | Handles the new combined message, the older spoof-only message, silence, server errors and contact confirmation |
| Privacy | Footer and subtitle say audio is processed in memory and text is not stored |

I syntax-checked both files with a JSX and CSS parser, but I haven't rendered the page, and I haven't seen your `App.css`, so the new panels may need small spacing or color tweaks.

## Complete `App.jsx`

```jsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import "./App.css";

const API_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8000";
const WS_URL = "ws://127.0.0.1:8000/ws";

const LABELS = ["urgency", "authority", "otp_pii", "payment"];
const LABEL_NAMES = {
  urgency: "Urgency",
  authority: "Authority claim",
  otp_pii: "OTP / PIN / card request",
  payment: "Payment request",
};

export default function App() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("Disconnected");
  const [score, setScore] = useState(null);
  const [latency, setLatency] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [recording, setRecording] = useState(false);
  const [fileResult, setFileResult] = useState(null);
  const [fileLoading, setFileLoading] = useState(false);
  const [fileError, setFileError] = useState("");

  // Live analysis (spoof + speech + scam signals + voice check)
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState(null);
  const [silent, setSilent] = useState(false);
  const [contacts, setContacts] = useState([]);
  const [contact, setContact] = useState("");

  const wsRef = useRef(null);
  const audioContextRef = useRef(null);
  const sourceRef = useRef(null);
  const processorRef = useRef(null);
  const streamRef = useRef(null);
  const contactRef = useRef("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadContacts();
    return () => {
      stopAudio();
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  async function loadContacts() {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/contacts`);
      const data = await response.json();
      setContacts(data.contacts || []);
    } catch {
      // Backend not reachable yet, or it has no /contacts endpoint. The voice check simply stays unavailable.
    }
  }

  function sendContact(name) {
    const ws = wsRef.current;
    if (ws && ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
      ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "select_contact", name: name || null }));
    }
  }

  function onContactChange(event) {
    const name = event.target.value;
    setContact(name);
    contactRef.current = name;
    sendContact(name);
  }

  async function startAudio() {
    try {
      setError("");
      setScore(null);
      setLatency(null);
      setMsg(null);
      setSilent(false);
      setStatus("Requesting microphone…");
      loadContacts();

      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        audio: {
          channelCount: 1,
          echoCancellation: false,
          noiseSuppression: false,
          autoGainControl: false,
        },
      });
      streamRef.current = stream;

      const audioContext = new AudioContext({ sampleRate: 16000 });
      audioContextRef.current = audioContext;

      console.log("Browser audio sample rate:", audioContext.sampleRate);

      const ws = new WebSocket(WS_URL);
      ws.binaryType = "arraybuffer";
      wsRef.current = ws;

      ws.onopen = async () => {
        try {
          setStatus("Connected — listening");
          setRecording(true);
          sendContact(contactRef.current);

          const source = audioContext.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
          sourceRef.current = source;

          await audioContext.audioWorklet.addModule("/processor.js");

          const processor = new AudioWorkletNode(
            audioContext,
            "pcm-processor"
          );
          processorRef.current = processor;

          processor.port.onmessage = (event) => {
            if (ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;

            ws.send(event.data);
          };

          source.connect(processor);

          // Keep the audio graph active without playing microphone audio
          // through the speakers.
          const mute = audioContext.createGain();
          mute.gain.value = 0;
          processor.connect(mute);
          mute.connect(audioContext.destination);
        } catch (err) {
          setError(err.message || "Could not initialize audio processing.");
          setStatus("Unable to start");
          await stopAudio();
        }
      };

      ws.onerror = () => {
        setError("WebSocket connection failed. Check that FastAPI is running.");
        setStatus("Connection error");
      };

      ws.onclose = () => {
        setStatus("Disconnected");
        setRecording(false);
      };

      ws.onmessage = (event) => {
        try {
          const result = JSON.parse(event.data);

          if (result.type === "error") {
            setError(`Server: ${result.detail}`);
            return;
          }
          if (result.type === "contact_set") return;
          if (result.silent) {
            setSilent(true);
            return;
          }

          if (result.streams) {
            // Unified message: spoof + intent (+ voice)
            setSilent(false);
            setError("");
            setMsg(result);
            setScore(result.streams.spoof?.score ?? null);
            setLatency(result.latency_ms?.total ?? null);
          } else if (result.stream === "spoof") {
            // Older spoof-only message, still supported
            setScore(result.score);
            setLatency(result.latency_ms);
          }
        } catch {
          setError("Received an invalid response from the backend.");
        }
      };
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Could not start microphone.");
      setStatus("Unable to start");
      await stopAudio();
    }
  }

  
  async function stopAudio() {
    setRecording(false);

    if (processorRef.current) {
      processorRef.current.port.onmessage = null;
      processorRef.current.disconnect();
      processorRef.current = null;
    }

    if (sourceRef.current) {
      sourceRef.current.disconnect();
      sourceRef.current = null;
    }

    if (wsRef.current) {
      wsRef.current.onclose = null;

      if (
        wsRef.current.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN ||
        wsRef.current.readyState === WebSocket.CONNECTING
      ) {
        wsRef.current.close();
      }

      wsRef.current = null;
    }

    if (streamRef.current) {
      streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
      streamRef.current = null;
    }

    if (audioContextRef.current) {
      await audioContextRef.current.close().catch(() => {});
      audioContextRef.current = null;
    }

    setStatus("Disconnected");
  }

  
  async function analyzeFile(event) {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setFileResult(null);
    setFileError("");

    
    if (!/\.(wav|ogg|oga|opus)$/i.test(file.name)) {
      setFileError("Please select a WAV, OGG, OGA, or OPUS audio file.");
      event.target.value = "";
      return;
    }


    setFileLoading(true);

    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append("file", file);

      const response = await fetch(
        `${API_URL}/analyze-file`,
        {
          method: "POST",
          body: formData,
        }
      );

      const data = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(data.detail || "Audio analysis failed.");
      }

      setFileResult(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setFileError(
        err.message || "Could not connect to the backend."
      );
    } finally {
      setFileLoading(false);
      event.target.value = "";
    }
  }

  const percent = score == null ? null : Math.round(score * 10000) / 100;

  const intent = msg?.streams?.intent;
  const voice = msg?.streams?.voice;
  const fired = new Set(intent?.evidence || []);
  const lat = msg?.latency_ms;
  const voiceMismatch = !!voice?.evidence?.length;
  const voicePercent = voice ? Math.round(voice.score * 100) : 0;

  return (
    <main className="app">
      <header className="header">
        <div className="brand">
          <div className="brand-icon">RV</div>
          <div>
            <h1>RakshaVoice</h1>
            <p>Real-time scam-call and voice monitoring</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className={`status ${recording ? "active" : ""}`}>
          <span className="status-dot" />
          {status}
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className="hero">
        <p className="eyebrow">AUDIO SECURITY · LIVE ANALYSIS</p>
        <h2>Detect suspicious calls in real time.</h2>
        <p className="subtitle">
          Listens to the call through your microphone, checks whether the voice
          is synthetic, reads what is being asked, and compares the voice with
          a person you have enrolled. Audio is processed in memory on this
          computer and discarded.
        </p>
      </section>

      <section className="dashboard">
        <article className="panel score-panel">
          <p className="panel-label">LATEST SPOOF SCORE</p>
          <div className="score">
            {percent == null ? "—" : `${percent.toFixed(2)}%`}
          </div>
          <p className="muted">
            {score == null
              ? "Start monitoring to receive a score"
              : score >= 0.5
                ? "Higher predicted spoof probability"
                : "Lower predicted spoof probability"}
          </p>
          <div className="meter">
            <div
              className="meter-fill"
              style={{ width: `${percent == null ? 0 : percent}%` }}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="meter-labels">
            <span>0% · Lower</span>
            <span>100% · Higher</span>
          </div>
        </article>

        <article className="panel">
          <p className="panel-label">INFERENCE LATENCY</p>
          <div className="metric">
            {latency == null ? "—" : `${Number(latency).toFixed(1)} ms`}
          </div>
          <p className="muted">
            {lat
              ? `Spoof ${lat.spoof} · speech ${lat.asr} · intent ${lat.intent}${
                  lat.voice !== undefined ? ` · voice ${lat.voice}` : ""
                } ms`
              : "Time spent scoring the latest audio window"}
          </p>
        </article>

        <article className="panel">
          <p className="panel-label">CONNECTION</p>
          <div className="metric metric-small">
            {recording ? "Live" : "Idle"}
          </div>
          <p className="muted">
            Local FastAPI WebSocket
            {msg ? ` · skipped windows: ${msg.dropped_windows ?? 0}` : ""}
          </p>
          <p className="endpoint">{WS_URL}</p>
        </article>
      </section>

      <section className="panel controls">
        <div>
          <h3>Live microphone analysis</h3>
          <p className="muted">
            Allow microphone access when your browser asks. Keep speaking to
            receive successive audio-window scores.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="buttons">
          {!recording ? (
            <button className="primary" onClick={startAudio}>
              Start monitoring
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button className="danger" onClick={stopAudio}>
              Stop monitoring
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        {error && <p className="error">{error}</p>}
      </section>

      <section className="analysis-grid">
        <article className="panel">
          <p className="panel-label">WHAT THE CALLER IS SAYING</p>
          <div className="transcript">
            {msg?.transcript
              ? msg.transcript
              : recording
                ? silent
                  ? "Listening… (silence)"
                  : "Waiting for speech…"
                : "Start monitoring to see the live transcript."}
          </div>
          <p className="muted">
            Text is shown here only and is not stored.
          </p>

          <p className="panel-label signals-label">SCAM SIGNALS</p>
          <div className="chips">
            {LABELS.map((label) => {
              const value = intent?.labels?.[label];
              const on = fired.has(label);
              return (
                <span key={label} className={`chip ${on ? "on" : ""}`}>
                  {LABEL_NAMES[label]}
                  {value !== undefined ? ` · ${Math.round(value * 100)}%` : ""}
                </span>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </article>

        <article className="panel">
          <p className="panel-label">VOICE CHECK</p>
          <label className="select-label" htmlFor="contact">
            The caller says they are
          </label>
          <select
            id="contact"
            className="select"
            value={contact}
            onChange={onContactChange}
          >
            <option value="">Nobody in particular</option>
            {contacts.map((name) => (
              <option key={name} value={name}>
                {name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          {voice ? (
            <>
              <div className={`metric metric-small ${voiceMismatch ? "bad" : "good"}`}>
                {voiceMismatch ? "Voice does not match" : "Voice matches"}
              </div>
              <div className="meter">
                <div
                  className="meter-fill"
                  style={{
                    width: `${voicePercent}%`,
                    background: voiceMismatch ? "#e5484d" : "#30a46c",
                  }}
                />
              </div>
              <div className="meter-labels">
                <span>Same person</span>
                <span>Different person</span>
              </div>
              <p className="muted">
                Compared with “{voice.enrolled}” · similarity {voice.similarity}
              </p>
            </>
          ) : (
            <p className="muted">
              {contacts.length
                ? "Pick the person the caller claims to be, then keep speaking."
                : "No enrolled contacts yet. Enrol someone with the enrolment script first."}
            </p>
          )}
        </article>
      </section>

      
      <section className="panel controls file-analysis">
        <div>
          <h3>Analyze an audio file</h3>
          <p className="muted">
            Upload a WAV recording to analyze it with the trained
            RakshaVoice spoof classifier.
          </p>
        </div>

        <input
          type="file"
          accept=".wav,.ogg,.oga,.opus,audio/*"
          onChange={analyzeFile}
          disabled={fileLoading}
        />

        {fileLoading && <p>Analyzing audio...</p>}

        {fileError && <p className="error">{fileError}</p>}

        {fileResult && (
          <div className="file-result">
            <h3>Analysis result</h3>
            <p>
              <strong>File:</strong> {fileResult.filename}
            </p>
            <p>
              <strong>Duration:</strong>{" "}
              {fileResult.duration_seconds} seconds
            </p>
            <p>
              <strong>Spoof probability:</strong>{" "}
              {fileResult.spoof_probability_percent}%
            </p>
            <p>
              <strong>Sample rate:</strong>{" "}
              {fileResult.sample_rate} Hz
            </p>
            <p className="muted">
              Higher scores indicate higher predicted spoof probability.
              This is a model prediction, not a definitive verdict.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>


      <footer>
        <p>
          Scores are model predictions, not proof that a voice is authentic or
          spoofed. Validate against known audio before relying on decisions.
        </p>
        <p>RakshaVoice · Local prototype</p>
      </footer>
    </main>
  );
}
```

import asyncio, json, os, sys, time

if __package__ in (None, ""):
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

import numpy as np
from fastapi import FastAPI, WebSocket
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from audio.spoof_stream import score_chunk
from nlp.asr import transcribe
from nlp.intent_stream import score_text
from voice.voice_stream import score_voice, list_enrolled
from backend.chunker import RingBuffer

SILENCE_RMS = 0.005                      # windows quieter than this are not sent to any model
NEUTRAL_INTENT = {"stream": "intent", "score": 0.0, "labels": {}, "evidence": []}

app = FastAPI()
app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=["*"], allow_methods=["*"], allow_headers=["*"])


@app.get("/health")
def health():
    return {"ok": True}


@app.get("/contacts")
def contacts():
    return {"contacts": list_enrolled()}


def _timed(fn, *args):
    t0 = time.time()
    out = fn(*args)
    return out, int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)


def _intent_pipeline(window):
    text, t_asr = _timed(transcribe, window)
    if not text.strip():
        return text, NEUTRAL_INTENT, t_asr, 0
    res, t_int = _timed(score_text, text)
    return text, res, t_asr, t_int


async def _process(ws, window, state):
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        if float(np.sqrt(np.mean(window ** 2))) < SILENCE_RMS:
            await ws.send_json({"t": time.time(), "silent": True})
            return
        contact = state["contact"]
        jobs = [loop.run_in_executor(None, _timed, score_chunk, window),
                loop.run_in_executor(None, _intent_pipeline, window)]
        if contact:
            jobs.append(loop.run_in_executor(None, _timed, score_voice, window, contact))
        res = await asyncio.gather(*jobs)
        (spoof, t_spoof), (text, intent, t_asr, t_int) = res[0], res[1]
        streams = {"spoof": {"stream": "spoof", "score": round(float(spoof), 4)}, "intent": intent}
        lat = {"spoof": t_spoof, "asr": t_asr, "intent": t_int}
        if contact:
            voice, t_voice = res[2]
            streams["voice"] = voice
            lat["voice"] = t_voice
        lat["total"] = int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)
        await ws.send_json({"t": time.time(), "window_s": 4, "transcript": text, "streams": streams,
                            "latency_ms": lat, "dropped_windows": state["dropped"]})
    except Exception as e:                                   # never freeze the UI on a model error
        await ws.send_json({"type": "error", "detail": f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"})
    finally:
        state["busy"] = False


@app.websocket("/ws")
async def ws_endpoint(ws: WebSocket):
    await ws.accept()
    buf = RingBuffer()
    state = {"busy": False, "contact": None, "dropped": 0}
    task = None
    try:
        while True:
            m = await ws.receive()
            if m["type"] == "websocket.disconnect":
                break
            if m.get("bytes") is not None:
                window = buf.push(np.frombuffer(m["bytes"], dtype=np.float32))
                if window is None:
                    continue
                if state["busy"]:
                    state["dropped"] += 1                    # previous window still being scored: skip this one
                    continue
                state["busy"] = True
                task = asyncio.create_task(_process(ws, window, state))
            elif m.get("text") is not None:
                try:
                    ctrl = json.loads(m["text"])
                except ValueError:
                    continue
                if ctrl.get("type") == "select_contact":
                    name = ctrl.get("name") or None
                    if name is None or name in list_enrolled():
                        state["contact"] = name
                        await ws.send_json({"type": "contact_set", "name": name})
                    else:
                        await ws.send_json({"type": "error", "detail": f"'{name}' is not enrolled"})
    finally:
        if task is not None and not task.done():
            task.cancel()
        buf.clear()

## Done: the merged `backend/main.py`, with file upload restored

Your pasted file was my live-pipeline version with no `/analyze-file` endpoint. I can't see your old one, so I rebuilt it from what your page expects. It takes a multipart `file`, then returns `filename`, `duration_seconds`, `spoof_probability_percent` and `sample_rate`. Errors come back as `{"detail": ...}` with a 400 status, which your page already displays.

**What I tested** (with stand-in models, not your real ones): the upload endpoint on an 8 s WAV at 44.1 kHz, a 3 s OGG, a 20 s WAV, a silent file, a wrong extension, a corrupted WAV and a 0.2 s file, all as expected. I also re-ran the live WebSocket tests (stream message, contact selection, silence, skipped-window counter) against this merged file, and they still pass. Not tested: your real models, GPU, microphone, or the page itself.

**How the new upload scoring works** (a guess at your old behaviour, so check it):
- The file is read into memory, never saved to disk (consistent with your privacy design).
- It is converted to mono 16 kHz, cut into 4 s windows with a 2 s hop, and silent windows are skipped.
- `spoof_probability_percent` is the **average** over the windows, capped at 40 windows for long files.
- The response also contains `windows_scored` and `max_spoof_probability_percent`. Your page ignores them, but they are there if you want to display them.
- It rejects files over 25 MB, under 0.5 s, silent, or not WAV/OGG/OGA/OPUS.

If your old endpoint scored differently (for example only the first 4 seconds, or the whole file in one pass), tell me and I'll match it.

## Complete `backend/main.py`

```python
import asyncio, io, json, os, sys, time

if __package__ in (None, ""):
    sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

import librosa
import numpy as np
import soundfile as sf
from fastapi import FastAPI, File, HTTPException, UploadFile, WebSocket
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from audio.spoof_stream import score_chunk
from nlp.asr import transcribe
from nlp.intent_stream import score_text
from voice.voice_stream import score_voice, list_enrolled
from backend.chunker import RingBuffer

SILENCE_RMS = 0.005                      # windows quieter than this are not sent to any model
NEUTRAL_INTENT = {"stream": "intent", "score": 0.0, "labels": {}, "evidence": []}

ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = (".wav", ".ogg", ".oga", ".opus")
MAX_FILE_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024        # upload limit
MAX_FILE_WINDOWS = 40                    # at most this many 4 s windows are scored per file
WIN, HOP = 64000, 32000                  # 4 s window, 2 s hop at 16 kHz

app = FastAPI()
app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=["*"], allow_methods=["*"], allow_headers=["*"])


@app.get("/health")
def health():
    return {"ok": True}


@app.get("/contacts")
def contacts():
    return {"contacts": list_enrolled()}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- file analysis (kept in memory, never saved to disk)
def _analyze_audio(data: bytes, filename: str) -> dict:
    try:
        y, sr = sf.read(io.BytesIO(data), dtype="float32", always_2d=True)
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Could not read this audio file. Try a standard WAV file.")
    y = y.mean(axis=1)                                       # mono
    duration = len(y) / sr
    if duration < 0.5:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="The audio is too short to analyze (under 0.5 seconds).")
    if sr != 16000:
        y = librosa.resample(y, orig_sr=sr, target_sr=16000)

    if len(y) <= WIN:
        windows = [y]
    else:
        starts = list(range(0, len(y) - WIN + 1, HOP))
        if starts[-1] + WIN < len(y):
            starts.append(len(y) - WIN)                      # make sure the end of the file is covered
        windows = [y[s:s + WIN] for s in starts]
    voiced = [w for w in windows if float(np.sqrt(np.mean(w ** 2))) >= SILENCE_RMS]
    if not voiced:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No speech detected in this file.")
    if len(voiced) > MAX_FILE_WINDOWS:                       # keep long files fast: evenly spaced sample
        idx = np.linspace(0, len(voiced) - 1, MAX_FILE_WINDOWS).astype(int)
        voiced = [voiced[i] for i in idx]

    scores = [float(score_chunk(w)) for w in voiced]
    return {
        "filename": filename,
        "duration_seconds": round(duration, 2),
        "spoof_probability_percent": round(float(np.mean(scores)) * 100, 2),
        "sample_rate": int(sr),
        "windows_scored": len(scores),
        "max_spoof_probability_percent": round(float(np.max(scores)) * 100, 2),
    }


@app.post("/analyze-file")
async def analyze_file(file: UploadFile = File(...)):
    name = file.filename or "audio"
    if not name.lower().endswith(ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Please upload a WAV, OGG, OGA, or OPUS audio file.")
    data = await file.read()
    if len(data) > MAX_FILE_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail="File too large (limit 25 MB).")
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    return await loop.run_in_executor(None, _analyze_audio, data, name)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- live pipeline
def _timed(fn, *args):
    t0 = time.time()
    out = fn(*args)
    return out, int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)


def _intent_pipeline(window):
    text, t_asr = _timed(transcribe, window)
    if not text.strip():
        return text, NEUTRAL_INTENT, t_asr, 0
    res, t_int = _timed(score_text, text)
    return text, res, t_asr, t_int


async def _process(ws, window, state):
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    t0 = time.time()
    try:
        if float(np.sqrt(np.mean(window ** 2))) < SILENCE_RMS:
            await ws.send_json({"t": time.time(), "silent": True})
            return
        contact = state["contact"]
        jobs = [loop.run_in_executor(None, _timed, score_chunk, window),
                loop.run_in_executor(None, _intent_pipeline, window)]
        if contact:
            jobs.append(loop.run_in_executor(None, _timed, score_voice, window, contact))
        res = await asyncio.gather(*jobs)
        (spoof, t_spoof), (text, intent, t_asr, t_int) = res[0], res[1]
        streams = {"spoof": {"stream": "spoof", "score": round(float(spoof), 4)}, "intent": intent}
        lat = {"spoof": t_spoof, "asr": t_asr, "intent": t_int}
        if contact:
            voice, t_voice = res[2]
            streams["voice"] = voice
            lat["voice"] = t_voice
        lat["total"] = int((time.time() - t0) * 1000)
        await ws.send_json({"t": time.time(), "window_s": 4, "transcript": text, "streams": streams,
                            "latency_ms": lat, "dropped_windows": state["dropped"]})
    except Exception as e:                                   # never freeze the UI on a model error
        await ws.send_json({"type": "error", "detail": f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"})
    finally:
        state["busy"] = False


@app.websocket("/ws")
async def ws_endpoint(ws: WebSocket):
    await ws.accept()
    buf = RingBuffer()
    state = {"busy": False, "contact": None, "dropped": 0}
    task = None
    try:
        while True:
            m = await ws.receive()
            if m["type"] == "websocket.disconnect":
                break
            if m.get("bytes") is not None:
                window = buf.push(np.frombuffer(m["bytes"], dtype=np.float32))
                if window is None:
                    continue
                if state["busy"]:
                    state["dropped"] += 1                    # previous window still being scored: skip this one
                    continue
                state["busy"] = True
                task = asyncio.create_task(_process(ws, window, state))
            elif m.get("text") is not None:
                try:
                    ctrl = json.loads(m["text"])
                except ValueError:
                    continue
                if ctrl.get("type") == "select_contact":
                    name = ctrl.get("name") or None
                    if name is None or name in list_enrolled():
                        state["contact"] = name
                        await ws.send_json({"type": "contact_set", "name": name})
                    else:
                        await ws.send_json({"type": "error", "detail": f"'{name}' is not enrolled"})
    finally:
        if task is not None and not task.done():
            task.cancel()
        buf.clear()
```

File uploads need the `python-multipart` package. If you see an error about it, run `pip install python-multipart` (you probably have it already, since your old upload endpoint worked).

3. In `frontend/`: `npm run dev`, open the page in Chrome.
4. Test in this order:

   - **Live, no contact:** say "Please tell me the OTP you just received". The transcript should appear and the `OTP / PIN / card request` chip should turn red.
   - **Voice check:** pick "me" in the dropdown and speak (expect "Voice matches"), then play a different voice through your speakers (expect "does not match").
   - **Load:** speak continuously for 30 seconds and watch the "skipped windows" count.

## What your startup output shows

| Item | Reading |
|---|---|
| Server started, `/contacts` and `/analyze-file` returned 200 | The merged backend runs, and **file upload works** |
| "Could not parse CUDA device string 'cuda'… Falling back to device 0" | Harmless. The voice model asked for `cuda` and SpeechBrain picked your only GPU. To silence it, in `voice/voice_stream.py` change `run_opts={"device": DEV}` to `run_opts={"device": "cuda:0" if DEV == "cuda" else DEV}` |
| "It took around 3-4 seconds" | I'm reading this as the file upload (the last log line). That is plausible for a first call, since the GPU warms up. If you meant server startup time, tell me |
| **GPU memory: 3,679 of 4,096 MiB in use** | **Only about 420 MiB spare.** This is the risk to watch. Edge, WhatsApp and the NVIDIA overlay are still running in your process list |

**About that memory:** live speech makes the models allocate extra temporary memory beyond what you see at idle, so you may hit an out-of-memory error during the test. The server won't crash: it sends the error to the page, and you'd see a red `Server: …OutOfMemory…` line. Before testing, do this in order:
1. Close Edge (`msedge`, `msedgewebview2`) and WhatsApp, then run `nvidia-smi` again. I'd like to see it under about 3,400 MiB.
2. If it is still above about 3,500 MiB, move the small voice model to the CPU. Stop the server (Ctrl+C), then in PowerShell run `$env:RV_VOICE_DEVICE="cpu"` and start the server again in the same window. (This uses the device override from my earlier Step 4. If you didn't make that two-line edit in `voice/voice_stream.py`, tell me and I'll resend it.)

## Test A: latency and skipped windows (about 5 minutes)

1. With the server running, open a second terminal in `rakshavoice/frontend` and run `npm run dev`. Open the address it prints (usually `http://localhost:5173`) in **Chrome**.
2. In a third terminal, run `nvidia-smi -l 2`. It prints GPU memory every 2 seconds, so you can see the peak while speaking. Leave it running.
3. In the page, click **Start monitoring** and allow the microphone.
4. Speak **continuously for 30 seconds**. Read this aloud, repeating it if needed:
   *"Sir, I am calling from your bank. Please tell me the OTP you just received. Your account will be blocked today, so please act now."*
5. While speaking, watch the page: the transcript should appear after about 4-6 seconds, and chips like `OTP / PIN / card request` should turn red.
6. **At the 30-second mark, without clicking Stop, write down:**
   - the **INFERENCE LATENCY** number and the line under it (for example "Spoof 180 · speech 700 · intent 25 ms"),
   - the **skipped windows** number in the CONNECTION panel,
   - the highest memory value `nvidia-smi` printed,
   - any red error text on the page.
7. Click **Stop monitoring**, then press Ctrl+C in the `nvidia-smi` terminal.

**How to read it:** a total below about 2,000 ms is good, because the system produces a result every 2 seconds. Skipped windows of 0-2 are fine. If the skipped count climbs steadily, the pipeline is slower than real time and I'll tune it.

## Test B: voice check

**Enrolling yourself (you must not run the enrolment while the server holds the GPU).** In a terminal in `rakshavoice/`, do **one** of these:
- Stop the server (Ctrl+C) and run the next commands, then restart it afterwards, or
- Leave the server running and use `$env:RV_VOICE_DEVICE="cpu"` in the terminal where you run the enrolment, so it doesn't need GPU memory.

Then run:

Enter the name `me`, read the consent statement, type `YES`, and speak naturally for 8 seconds at each of the three prompts (read anything, in any language). You should see `Enrolled: ['me']`. Restart the server if you stopped it.

**Checking a match:**
1. Reload the web page. The dropdown **"The caller says they are"** should now list `me`. Select it.
2. Click **Start monitoring** and speak for about 10 seconds. The Voice check panel should say **"Voice matches"** with a low bar, and a similarity roughly in the **0.6-0.8** range (my earlier test on your clean clips gave 0.80). Write down the similarity.
3. Move about 1.5 m away from the laptop and speak again. I expect it to drop to roughly 0.4-0.6 (your far-field clips averaged 0.58) but still say "matches" because the threshold is 0.298. Write down that number too.

**Checking a mismatch (a different voice):**
- **Best:** ask another person to speak into the microphone for 10 seconds. Expect **"Voice does not match"** with a similarity below about 0.3.
- **If nobody is available:** open a LibriSpeech file from `voice/data/LibriSpeech/test-clean/` (any `.flac`) in a media player and play it through your speakers, then stay quiet while it plays. The microphone picks it up. Expect the same "does not match" message. Note that this tests a speaker-versus-speaker difference, not a voice clone (that comes in Phase 8).

Write down the similarity values you see in each case, and whether the message ever flickers between "matches" and "does not match" for the same speaker.

Two numbers I still need from you: the **skipped-windows count** after the 30 seconds, and the breakdown under the latency number ("Spoof … · speech … · intent … ms"). With 1.84 s total on a 2 s hop, any slow window gets skipped. If the skipped count was 0-2, leave it. If it keeps rising, the easy fix is a longer hop: in `backend/chunker.py` change `HOP = 2 * SR` to `HOP = 3 * SR` (one result every 3 s instead of 2 s).

## Why your own voice scores a high spoof probability

This is the limitation your Phase 1 results already showed, now visible live:

- Your spoof model was trained only on ASVspoof 2019. Its "real" class is clean studio recordings, and its "fake" class is output from specific voice generators. It scored well on that dataset (eval EER 2.6%).
- On anything that doesn't look like that data, it generalised poorly: on In-the-Wild it got **EER 33%** and AUC 0.74, and your earlier 20-versus-20 test gave AUC **0.515** with scores near 1.0. An AUC of 0.5 means the scores carry no information. Your live microphone is exactly that situation.
- The likely mechanism: the frozen wav2vec2 features carry information about the recording channel (microphone, room, noise, level). The head learned "channel unlike the studio recordings means not-real", so a laptop microphone looks "fake".

I believe that is the cause, but I haven't proven it, so test it. This diagnostic scores the same kinds of real human speech under different recording conditions. Save it as `audio/channel_check.py` and run `python -m audio.channel_check` from `rakshavoice/` (stop the server first, so the GPU has room). I syntax-checked it but couldn't run it here.

```python
import glob, os, random
import numpy as np, soundfile as sf, librosa
from audio.spoof_stream import score_chunk

SR = 16000
rng = np.random.RandomState(0); random.seed(0)

def crop4(y):
    n = 4 * SR
    if len(y) < n: y = np.pad(y, (0, n - len(y)))
    s = rng.randint(0, len(y) - n + 1)
    return y[s:s + n]

def noisy(y, snr_db):
    return (y + rng.randn(len(y)) * np.std(y) / (10 ** (snr_db / 20))).astype(np.float32)

def phone(y):                                              # narrow-band (8 kHz) plus light noise
    y = librosa.resample(librosa.resample(y, orig_sr=SR, target_sr=8000), orig_sr=8000, target_sr=SR)
    return noisy(y, 25)

def room(y, rt=0.3):                                       # crude reverb: convolve with a decaying-noise impulse response
    n = int(rt * SR); ir = rng.randn(n) * np.exp(-6.9 * np.arange(n) / n); ir[0] = 1.0
    out = np.convolve(y, ir / np.sqrt(np.sum(ir ** 2)))[:len(y)]
    return (out / (np.max(np.abs(out)) + 1e-9) * np.max(np.abs(y))).astype(np.float32)

def report(name, scores):
    s = np.array(scores)
    print(f"{name:40s} n={len(s):3d}  mean={s.mean():.2f}  median={np.median(s):.2f}  share above 0.5 = {np.mean(s > 0.5) * 100:3.0f}%")

# 1) clean human speech that the model never saw (LibriSpeech test-clean)
libri = []
for d in sorted(glob.glob("voice/data/LibriSpeech/test-clean/*")):
    f = random.choice(glob.glob(f"{d}/*/*.flac")); y, sr = sf.read(f, dtype="float32")
    libri.append(crop4(y if sr == SR else librosa.resample(y, orig_sr=sr, target_sr=SR)))
report("LibriSpeech clean (human)", [score_chunk(y) for y in libri])
report("LibriSpeech + 8 kHz phone-like", [score_chunk(phone(y)) for y in libri])
report("LibriSpeech + noise 15 dB", [score_chunk(noisy(y, 15)) for y in libri])
report("LibriSpeech + room reverb", [score_chunk(room(y)) for y in libri])

# 2) your own recordings (same human, two microphone distances)
def own(pattern):
    out = []
    for f in sorted(glob.glob(pattern)):
        y = librosa.load(f, sr=SR, mono=True)[0]
        for i in range(0, max(1, len(y) - 4 * SR + 1), 2 * SR):
            w = y[i:i + 4 * SR]
            if np.sqrt(np.mean(w ** 2)) > 0.005: out.append(score_chunk(w))
    return out
report("Your voice, near mic (clean clips)", own("nlp/test_clips/*__clean.wav"))
report("Your voice, far (far clips)", own("nlp/test_clips/*__far.wav"))

# 3) optional: put any synthetic / text-to-speech WAV files in audio/test_fake/ to compare
fake = []
for f in sorted(glob.glob("audio/test_fake/*.wav")):
    y = librosa.load(f, sr=SR, mono=True)[0]; fake.append(score_chunk(crop4(y)))
if fake: report("Synthetic speech (audio/test_fake)", fake)
```

**How to read the result:**

| What you see | Meaning | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| LibriSpeech clean is already high | The head fails on any human speech that isn't ASVspoof-like (a general generalisation failure) | Retrain with more varied real speech |
| LibriSpeech clean is low, but phone / noise / reverb push it up | It is sensitive to channel conditions | Retrain with channel augmentation |
| LibriSpeech is low and only your microphone clips are high | It is specific to your laptop microphone | Include recordings from your own setup in training |

If you can, add a few synthetic WAV files (any text-to-speech tool, a few in English and Hindi) to `audio/test_fake/`, so you can see whether fake speech scores *higher* than real speech under the same conditions. That ordering is the thing that matters for a detector.

## What to do about it

**1. Be honest in the page now (5 minutes).** Your page says "Higher predicted spoof probability" for any score above 0.5, which will mislead anyone watching the demo. In `App.jsx`, add this line directly after the `meter-labels` block in the first panel:
```jsx
          <p className="muted">
            Experimental: not yet calibrated for live microphone audio.
          </p>
```

**2. The real fix: retrain the head on data that matches how you will demo.** This is the biggest remaining technical risk, because your live demo plays a cloned voice through a phone speaker into the laptop microphone. Your Phase 1 data never covered that path. The plan (about 2-3 days):
- **Real speech, varied:** LibriSpeech, your own recordings, and any Indian-language speech you can get, each with noise, phone-band and reverb versions.
- **Fake speech:** ASVspoof fakes (you have them) plus text-to-speech samples in English and Hindi from free tools.
- **A replay set:** play a few hundred real and synthetic clips from a phone speaker and record them through the laptop microphone, exactly as in the demo.
- Extract wav2vec2 features for the new data on Kaggle, retrain the head on the mixture, and report EER separately per condition and on a text-to-speech system not used in training. That last test is the "unseen cloning tool" evaluation your synopsis promises.

Without this fix, the spoof stream is nearly uninformative live, the fusion network will learn to ignore it, and the demo's AI-clone scenario won't work.
